I'll just stand in the kitchen eating takeout straight from the container, even though I'm not actually hungry. It's just very inconsistent.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any unusual thoughts or heard any voices that others couldn't hear during these past three months?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't.
